📜 VERSION LOG ENTRY (Ver 1.01):
## [Ver 1.01] - 2026-07-15 | Batch 0051–0055 Added & A/B Algorithm Test Initiated

### 🎯 Task Summary & Strategy:
* Added **5 new song optimization packages (`0051` to `0055`)** into the `bands/` directory, expanding the canonical workspace database to **55 completed songs**.
* **Song 0051 (`Self VS. Self` - Pendulum Feat. In Flames):** Fully optimized as a high-speed (175 BPM) drum & bass / melodic death metal pick-endurance workout. **Status:** Active upload shared directly to subscribers (with "publish to subscription feed and notify subscribers" enabled).
* **Song 0052 (`Bad Guy` - Billie Eilish):** Fully optimized as an organic Thunderbird adaptation of FINNEAS's 2019 Grammy-winning synth-bass groove. **Status:** Active upload released simultaneously with 0051 as a **Critical A/B Algorithm Test** — explicitly withheld from subscriber notifications (unchecked "publish to subscription feed and notify subscribers"). This tests how fast YouTube's pure non-subscriber Browse/Search/Shorts Feed algorithm identifies and pushes a mainstream pop hit compared to a subscriber-notified metal/DnB upload.
* **Songs 0053 (`9,000 Miles` - Pendulum), 0054 (`Bohemian Like You` - The Dandy Warhols), and 0055 (`Pizza` - Attila):** Fully researched and optimized as **YouTube Studio Drafts** queued to feed the algorithm sequentially as initial uploads gain traction.

### 🐍 Python Scripts & Bash Commands Used:
1. **`recover_mavo.py` (v5):** Bootstrapped the initial 50-song workspace, rebuilt `Titles.txt`, `Descriptions.txt`, `Pinned_Comments.txt`, and `Tags.txt`, and audited for zero repetition. Patched `write_optimization_md` to automatically decode UTF-16 surrogate pairs (`\ud83c\udfb8` → `🎸`) to prevent UTF-8 encoding crashes.
2. **`build_0051_0055.py`:** Created dictionary entries and directory structures for songs 0051–0055 under `bands/`, executed `recover_mavo.write_optimization_md(s)` for each new song, appended all 5 songs to `recover_mavo.SONGS`, and triggered `recover_mavo.rebuild_refs()`.
3. **`Section 9.6` Verification Block:** Executed via bash (`python3 -c "import re, glob..."`) across all 55 songs. Verified 100% unique Long-Form and Shorts title opening words, unique description first lines, unique pinned comments, and unique gear configurations (`55 -> all unique ✓`).
4. **Master Script Synchronizer (`recover_mavo.py` update):** Replaced the `SONGS = [...]` list in `recover_mavo.py` with the complete 55-song JSON array (`python3 -c "import recover_mavo, json..."`) so that running `python3 recover_mavo.py` in a virgin workspace instantly generates all 55 completed song folders with zero manual intervention.

### 📝 Files Created & Modified:
* **Created:** `bands/P/Pendulum_Feat._In_Flames/0051_Self_VS._Self/optimization.md`
* **Created:** `bands/B/Billie_Eilish/0052_Bad_Guy/optimization.md`
